Two-zone layout: a dark slate panel fills the left 35% of the slide with the title at top-left, and a full-bleed grid of eight street-style photographs occupies the right 65%, arranged in four columns by two rows. Title left-aligned at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6.4% from the top; footer line sits at the bottom-left margin at about 95.5% height, with the source line starting at about 27.4% across.

**Visuals**

Eight street-style photographs in a boxed 4x2 grid, with no gaps and no borders, cropped at the slide edges. Top row, left to right: (1) a bearded man with dark-rimmed glasses and a teal neckerchief wearing a sage-green button-front overshirt with a white paper in his hand, walking on a city street with a yellow kerb line; (2) a fair-haired man in round tinted sunglasses wearing a navy overcoat over an olive shirt and a striped top with beige chinos, hands in pockets, standing before a stone wall; (3) a dark-haired woman in a black leather biker jacket, striped top, and black skinny trousers walking on a wet pavement with stone steps behind; (4) a partly visible curly-haired woman in black leather against a white van, cropped at the right edge. Bottom row, left to right: (5) a woman with a white and navy striped fur-look jacket over an olive top and beige trousers, holding a brown tote bag on a city street; (6) a man with dark-rimmed glasses wearing an olive military-style field jacket over a light blue shirt and white trousers, holding a turquoise bag; (7) a bearded man in a black quilted-panel leather biker jacket with a black quilted waist bag and tan trousers; (8) a partly visible woman with sunglasses and a camel coat, cropped at the right edge. Overall lighting is natural daylight; dominant colours are sage green, navy, olive, black, white, and tan; photos are muted and desaturated.

**Design**

Background: Solid slate #40484F on the left panel and behind the slide, with the photo grid placed over the right 65%. Dark slate background #40484F for the left panel and the slide. Title text white #FFFFFF in a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light), about 5.9% of slide height for the cap-height-based size, single weight, sentence case, with no accents. Footer and source text are small white sans-serif (Proxima Nova Regular) at about 1.2% of slide height. Photos are unframed rectangles with no shadows, rounded corners or gradients. The slide has no accent colour beyond the photographs.

### Slide 53

**Text**

Automated process / analysis? / ©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential. / Source: Drishti

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-height dark slate panel fills the left 35% of the slide and a full-bleed photograph fills the right 65%. The title sits top-left in the panel at about 6.4% from the top and 3.4% from the left margin, left-aligned. The footer runs along the bottom of the panel: copyright text at far left and source credit at right, both at about 95.5% from the top. There is no slide number and no logo.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed in the right 65% of the slide with no border, overlay or tint. Subject: a worker in a blue work shirt with a face-obscuring pixelated blur, a grey ID badge on a clip and lanyard, and black gloves, seen from a high overhead angle. The worker is using a blue-handled screwdriver to fasten a component inside a grey metal chassis tray on a black workbench. Other objects: a silver power supply unit with multicoloured cables in the tray, a blue foam pad on the bench, a black 250GB hard drive with yellow label at lower right, a second hard drive and server boards at upper right, a black trash bin lined with a plastic bag at lower left, a pegboard with tools and blue-handled screwdrivers at top, and green and blue floor tape marking the work area. Lighting is even, bright overhead daylight. Dominant colours are blue, black, slate grey and light grey. The image is cropped to the right panel and runs to the top and bottom edges.

**Design**

Background is slate grey #40484F across the left panel with the photograph on the right. “Driverless car” = / “horseless carriage” / Getting rid of horses / changed everything / ©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential. / Source: Daimler

One photograph, a black-and-white vintage photograph of an early horseless carriage (a Daimler motor carriage). It shows a black enclosed closed-body coach with two glazed side doors, a black roof with a small window, a dashboard with a small rectangular plate, brass-coloured headlamps at the front, a horizontal cylindrical tank or silencer at the front-right, and four large spoked wheels with pale rims. A bare tree, a building facade and a white fence sit in the blurred background. The subject is shown in three-quarter view from the front-left, with the carriage facing right. Composition: the photo is placed in the right white area, not full-bleed; its edges are feathered into a soft white vignette rather than boxed by a frame. Lighting is soft, diffuse, overcast daylight with no hard shadows. Dominant colours are black, grey and white. No overlay or tint. The image is not cropped in the traditional sense because the vignette fades it out at the edges.

Title text is white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light, a geometric humanist sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height per line, with a light weight and normal tracking. Body text is white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular, a sans-serif, about 3.95% of slide height, with normal tracking and roughly 1.0 line spacing. The footer and source text are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular at about 4.4% of slide height, appearing dimmed. There are no visible accent colours and no divider lines. Shapes are plain rectangles with hard edges.

### Slide 59

**Text**

How much does automated driving change cities?
What changes when we automate driving and packetise roads?
No more
accidents
No more
parking
What happens
to congestion,
traffic signs,
road pricing?
Where do you
live? Eat?
Shop? Drink?
©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential.

**Layout**

A single full-width row of four equal circles sits in the vertical middle of the slide, separated by two grey plus signs and one grey arrow, with a left-aligned title block at the top and a footer line at bottom left. Title starts at x 3.4% and subtitle at x 3.5%, both with a left margin of about 3.5% of the slide width. The circles span x 2.9% to 95.8% and are vertically centred at about y 55.6%. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Four solid filled circles of equal size (each about 18.1% of slide width by 32.2% of slide height, so roughly circular) in a horizontal row, left to right: a steel-blue circle (#6C96B2) with the label 'No more accidents'; a magenta circle (#BE4681) with 'No more parking'; a lime-green circle (#95CA53) with 'What happens to congestion, traffic signs, road pricing?'; and an amber circle (#CB8B03) with 'Where do you live? Eat? Shop? Drink?'. Between the first and second circles and between the second and third sits a grey bold plus sign (about #848C96), each about 2.7% wide by 5.2% tall, centred vertically on the circles. Between the third and fourth circle sits a grey right-pointing block arrow (about #848C96), about 4.2% wide by 7.8% tall, centred vertically on the circles. The copyright line at bottom left is a raster image 305 by 26 pixels with light grey-white text.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Flat dark slate background (#40484F) with no gradient, texture or shadow. Title is white (#FFFFFF) in a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light), about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is amber-yellow (#FCB316) in the same light sans-serif at about 4.0% of slide height. Circle labels are white (#FFFFFF) in Proxima Nova Regular at about 3.5% of slide height, centred. Circles are flat fills with no outline and no shadow. Plus signs and arrow are flat grey with no outline. Accent colours are the four circle fills and the amber subtitle.

### Slide 60

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A single centred quotation over a contrasting caption lets one idea land on an otherwise empty slide.
- Review: The system holds from start to finish: dark slate ground, orange and blue accents, gold subtitles, consistent chart styling with sources, and full-bleed or half-slide photos that carry a point, such as the washing machine versus robot pair and the Wal-Mart photo on slide 60.

**Text**

Automatic cars change cities as much as cars changed cities / “It was easy to predict mass car-ownership but hard to predict Wal-Mart” / Source: Wal-mart

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top of the slide (about 22% height) holding the title and a quote subtitle left-aligned at a left margin of about 3.4% of slide width; a full-bleed photograph fills the remaining lower ~78% edge to edge. The source line sits bottom-right over the photo; no slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed, spanning the full width below the header band. Subject: a 1960s-70s era Wal-Mart storefront with a row of large white block letters reading W A L M A R T on a flat low building roof, with a 'DISCOUNT CITY' sign in red/orange capitals at the right end. In front is a parking lot with several period cars (a white pickup truck at left, dark sedans and a dark coupe in the centre, a row of parked cars at right) and a tall light pole; a bare tree sits behind the roof and a grass verge and road run along the bottom. Lighting: bright daylight under a clear pale blue sky. Composition: horizontal, eye-level, subject centred with the building spanning the width; the photo is slightly soft and warm-toned with faded colour, dominant colours blue sky, grey-white roof, dark car bodies and green-grey grass. Not cropped beyond the frame; no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Slate grey #40484F header band over a full-bleed photograph of the Wal-Mart storefront and parking lot. Header band in dark slate grey #40484F across the top ~22% of the slide, flat with no gradient or shadow. Title in white #FFFFFF, ProximaNova Light (geometric sans-serif, regular light weight), sentence case, size about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle quote in amber-yellow #FCB316, same ProximaNova Light family, about 4.0% of slide height, with curly quotation marks. Body/furniture text: source line in small white light sans-serif. Accent colour amber #FCB316. No shapes, lines, or corner radii.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

**Text**

Mixed reality 2017 =
Multi-touch 2006
Primary technology
Working
Part of the future
Not a commercial
product - yet
©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential.
Source: TED

**Layout**

Two-column split: a dark slate panel occupies the left 35% of the slide width and holds the title and bullet-style lines, left-aligned at about 3.4% from the left edge. A full-height photograph fills the right 65% edge to edge. The title sits at the top of the panel (about 6.4% from the top), the four statements run in a single stacked column from about 37% to 79% of the height, and a small footer line sits along the bottom of the panel at about 95.5% of the height. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed in the right 65% of the slide (x 34.9 to 100, full height, not boxed, no overlay or tint, not cropped at the edges beyond the frame). Subject: a man in a black turtleneck with glasses and a headset microphone stands behind a tilted, black-framed clear acrylic display on a black metal stand; the display shows a blue sky with bright white and cream clouds, lit from the upper left. In front of him a second man in dark clothing, with a lanyard badge, holds a shoulder-mounted video camera pointed at the viewer's left. Beneath the display is a black box, and at the bottom left a grey metal panel with a small black camera sensor sits on the stage floor with cables. Lighting: strong red stage lighting with a warm red-orange glow on the backdrop and a dim pink spot at the right. Dominant colours: saturated red #C0141C, near-black #101010, sky blue #2A5FD0, cloud white #F4EBDD. Composition: the subject is centred slightly right, with the display in the lower middle and the stage backdrop filling the upper area. No charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a flat dark slate panel (#40484F) on the left 35% of the slide, with the photograph on the right 65%. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in a light geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova-Light style), about 5.9% of slide height, set in two lines. Body text is white (#FFFFFF) in ProximaNova-Regular, about 4.4% of slide height, with generous vertical gaps between statements and no bullet markers. Footer text is light grey (#D0D4D8) in a small sans-serif, roughly 1.2% of slide height, rendered as an image-based line. No accent colour, no shapes with rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 64

**Text**

Mixed reality 2017 = / Multi-touch 2006 / Primary technology / Working / Part of the future / Not a commercial / product - yet / ©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential. / Source: Magic Leap

**Layout**

Two-column split: a dark slate panel fills the left 35% of the slide with the title and four-line body list stacked top-left at a 3.4% left margin, and a full-bleed photograph fills the right 65%. The title sits at the top of the panel, the body list sits in the middle-to-lower panel, and a footer row runs along the bottom of the panel with the copyright line at left and the source credit at right. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph filling the right 65% of the slide (x 35 to 100, full height, full-bleed, unboxed). Subject: two children seated at a table by a bright window. In the foreground at right, a boy in a striped long-sleeved top is seen from behind and partly cropped by the right and bottom edges, turned toward the girl. In the centre, a smiling girl with straight shoulder-length black hair and blunt bangs, wearing a cream top, presses her palms together in front of her chest. Two translucent, digitally rendered seahorses float in the air: a larger pink-and-orange seahorse at left, its tail curling down to the bottom edge of the frame, and a smaller lavender-blue seahorse at upper centre-right with a curled tail. A pencil lies on the table at lower right. Lighting is soft, diffuse daylight from the window, which blows out to near-white at the top centre. Dominant colours: white and pale grey from the window and walls, pink, magenta and orange for the large seahorse, lavender blue for the small one, cream for the girl's top, navy and white stripes for the boy's shirt. Composition is a close, slightly low frame with the girl at centre and the boy cropped at right. The photo is not overlaid or tinted. Small footer images: a 305 by 26 pixel picture at x 2.9, y 95.5 carries the copyright line; a 146 by 52 pixel picture at x 29.4, y 95.5 carries the source credit; a 112 by 52 pixel picture at x 26.7, y 95.5 sits behind or beside it with no separate visible content.

**Design**

Background is white with a dark slate #40484F panel covering the left 35% of the slide. The title and body text are white #FFFFFF; the footer text is light grey-white, about #E6E9EC, at small size. No accent colours, no shapes, lines, gradients, shadows or rounded corners. Typeface is Proxima Nova (a geometric sans-serif): Light weight for the title, Regular weight for the body list and footer. Title text is about 5.9% of slide height and body text about 4.4% of slide height. Text is sentence case with no capitals transforms and no letter-spacing changes.

Item lines in ProximaNova Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 4.0% of slide height, centred. Accents are the orange #D9822B and steel-blue #6A97B0 circle outlines and the grey #A6A6A6 arrow. ### Slide 69

**Text**

So, if the internet / automated this…

**Layout**

Two-column split: a dark slate left column about 35% of the slide width holds the title at top-left and a small copyright footer at bottom-left, with about 3.4% left margin. The black-and-white photograph fills the right 65% of the slide full-bleed from top to bottom. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One black-and-white archival photograph occupying the right 65% of the slide (x 35 to 100, full height), full-bleed with no border. Subject: an early-20th-century office or filing hall with a tall wall of small cabinet drawers with label holders and finger holes, and two men in dark suits seated in enclosed wooden desk booths that project from the wall on vertical rails. The upper man sits beside a black desk lamp reading or writing; the lower man sits at a desk with papers and his hands on the work surface, looking down. Tall multi-pane windows at upper right, a radiator at right, a tiled floor at the bottom, and a vertical metal lift column at right. Composition: the wall of drawers runs diagonally from lower left to upper right, and the men are placed in the centre-right. Lighting: diffuse daylight from the windows, mid-grey to near-black tones with bright highlights on the windows and walls. Dominant colours: greyscale only. The photo is cropped at all four edges of its frame and sits over the right side of the dark panel.

**Design**

Background: Dark slate #40484F panel on the left 35% of the slide, with the black-and-white photograph covering the right 65%. Two-tone slide: a flat dark slate panel (#40484F) on the left, with a black-and-white photograph covering the right side. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light), about 5.9% of slide height, set in regular case with no letter-spacing changes, in two lines with comfortable leading. The footer is a tiny white sans-serif line. No accent colours, shapes, lines, rules or shadows; no corner radii or gradients.

### Slide 70

**Text**

And this… / ©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column split with a dark slate panel occupying the left 35% of the slide and a full-bleed black-and-white photograph filling the right 65%. The title sits top-left at about 6.4% from the top and 3.4% from the left edge, on one line. The copyright footer sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top, left-aligned with the title. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One black-and-white photograph in the right 65% of the slide, full-height and full-bleed to the top, bottom and right edges, with its left edge abutting the slate panel at x 35%. Subject: three women at a vintage manual telephone switchboard, a wall of patch panels with jacks and dense bundles of cables. The woman in the foreground on the right, wearing a headset with a headband and a light top, is reaching up with her right arm extended to plug a cable into the panel. A second woman in the middle wears headphones and a striped blouse, facing the panel. A third woman is partly visible at the far left with one arm raised to the panel. Lighting is soft and directional from the upper left, giving high contrast with bright highlights on the blouses and deep shadows in the lower right. Dominant colours are greys and black, with near-white highlights. The image is cropped at the slide edges and not boxed, with no overlay or tint. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a solid dark slate panel #40484F covering the left 35% of the slide, with the photograph filling the right side. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Light (geometric humanist sans-serif, light weight, sentence case), about 5.9% of slide height. Footer text is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Regular, small, about 1.5% of slide height (estimated). No accent colour is used. Shapes have square corners, no borders, no shadows and no gradients.

### Slide 71

**Text**

Now ‘crypto’ / automates another / analogue / technology / ©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a dark slate panel fills the left 35% of the slide with the title in the upper left, and a full-height image fills the right 65% edge to edge. The title starts about 3.4% from the left edge and 6.4% from the top, and the small footer line sits near the bottom left at about 95.5% from the top. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One decorative illustration occupying the right 65% of the slide, full-bleed from top to bottom and cropped at the slide edges. It shows a repeating security-banknote style guilloche pattern: interlocking ornamental diamonds with scrolled tendrils and leaf motifs in mauve and dusky-rose line work, overlaid on fine concentric arc and wave textures in peach, grey and brown, with a field of mesh-like rosette lattice in magenta and teal at the right edge, a pale blue and green arabesque pattern at the top left, and bold white curved bands that form chevron-like shapes. Composition is dense and symmetrical around a central diamond motif, with no people or objects. Colours are soft pastels: peach #F9D9A8, light blue #A9C8E8, sage green #B9D6A0, mauve #B5607E, dusty rose #C98A8A, magenta #B33D7E, white #FFFFFF. No overlay, tint or gradient is visible. The footer line is a small image of text, not a logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid slate #40484F on the left panel, with the full-bleed patterned image covering the right 65% of the slide. Flat, minimal look with a dark slate left panel contrasting against the colourful pattern on the right. Title text is white, set in a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light style), regular weight, sentence case, at about 5.9% of slide height per line with generous line spacing (about 7.2% of slide height between baselines). Footer text is white, very small, light sans-serif. Accent colours come only from the image. No shapes have corner radii, shadows or gradients; the panel edge is a hard vertical boundary at 35% of the width.

### Slide 72

**Text**

Money in the cloud for centuries, but it was never software
Money went to the
cloud, then to
paper, then to
databases
Contracts, bonds,
stocks, deeds and
every kind of
written trust follows
All stored in
databases
But all as inert as
gold or paper
©2017 Andreessen Horowitz. Proprietary and confidential.

**Layout**

Single-row three-column layout of equal circles with arrows between them, all centred vertically in the middle band of the slide. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top with a left margin of about 3.4%. The footer wordmark sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Three hollow circles in a horizontal row, each with a text label centred inside. Left circle outlined in orange, middle circle in muted blue, right circle in green, each with a stroke about 0.4% of slide width. Two solid right-pointing block arrows in grey sit in the gaps between the circles, pointing from left circle to middle circle and from middle circle to right circle. No photographs, charts or logos other than the small footer wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Flat dark slate background with no gradient or texture. Title and body text are white, set in a geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova style). Title is Light weight at about 5.9% of slide height in sentence case. Body text is Regular weight at about 3.5% of slide height, centred. Circle outlines are orange #D9822B, blue #6B98B5 and green #8CC63F. Arrows are grey #85929C with no outline. Circles have no fill and no shadow. Footer wordmark is small white text.
